+ "Creates an automation that initiates a general cleaning job for a robot vacuum or other cleaning device,\nbased on a specified time, recurring schedule, or event trigger (e.g., sunrise, sunset, arriving home,\nleaving home, specific days of the week)\nOr simply when the user says that it wants to create an automation that initiates a general cleaning job"
+ "Creates an automation that resumes a cleaning task for a robot vacuum or other cleaning device,\nbased on a specified time, recurring schedule, or event trigger (e.g., sunrise, sunset, arriving home,\nleaving home, specific days of the week)\nOr simply when the user says that it wants to create an automation that resumes a cleaning task for a\nrobot vacuum or other cleaning device"
+ "Creates an automation to activate a smart home scene based on a specified time, recurring schedule,\nor event trigger (e.g., sunrise, sunset, arriving home, leaving home, specific days of the week)\nOr simply when the user says that it wants to create an automation to run a scene"
+ "Creates an automation to adjust the brightness of a smart light,\nbased on a specified time, recurring schedule, or event trigger (e.g., sunrise, sunset, arriving home,\nleaving home, specific days of the week)\nOr simply when the user says that it wants to create an automation to set the brightness value"
+ "Creates an automation to adjust the climate mode, allowing them to switch between modes\nsuch as heat, cool, auto, on/off,\nbased on a specified time, recurring schedule, or event trigger (e.g., sunrise, sunset, arriving home,\nleaving home, specific days of the week)\nOr simply when the user says that it wants to create an automation to set the climate mode"
+ "Creates an automation to adjust the operating mode of an air purifier,\nsuch as auto or manual,\nbased on a specified time, recurring schedule, or event trigger (e.g., sunrise, sunset, arriving home,\nleaving home, specific days of the week)\nOr simply when the user says that it wants to create an automation to adjust the operating mode\nof an air purifier"
+ "Creates an automation to adjust the position of a device that support the position capability,\nsuch as window blinds or shades,\nbased on a specified time, recurring schedule, or event trigger (e.g., sunrise, sunset, arriving home,\nleaving home, specific days of the week)\nOr simply when the user says that it wants to create an automation to adjust the position of a device"
+ "Creates an automation to change the color of a smart light,\nbased on a specified time, recurring schedule, or event trigger (e.g., sunrise, sunset, arriving home,\nleaving home, specific days of the week)\nOr simply when the user says that it wants to create an automation to set the color value"
+ "Creates an automation to change the operating mode of a humidifier or dehumidifier,\nsuch as humidify, dehumidify or auto,\nbased on a specified time, recurring schedule, or event trigger (e.g., sunrise, sunset, arriving home,\nleaving home, specific days of the week)\nOr simply when the user says that it wants to create an automation to change the operating mode of a\nhumidifier or dehumidifier"
